Ozma in The Hungry Tiger of Oz

Ruth Plumly Thompson · 1926

Ozma is the little, gentle fairy Princess and Queen of Oz, ruling all four countries from the Royal Palace. Soft-hearted and able, she is trusted to help others, has banished Ruggedo to a lonely isle, can restore Prince Evered to Rash's throne, and carries the Wizard's wishing powders, a magic belt, an emerald pin, and a ribbon. At Betsy's birthday party she hosts kindly, restrains teasing, and mistakes a flash for a coming thunderstorm. While searching for Betsy, Atmos Fere abducts her from a garden and carries her into the sky. Ozma punctures him with her pin, falls with him, saves him by tying her ribbon around his neck, and helps Rusty Ore manage his inflation. She befriends Atmos, travels through storms, a dog attack, and the rolling eastern country, and becomes lost with him before rejoining Betsy and the others. She praises Reddy, proposes consulting the Wizard to restore him, witnesses Rash's conquest, and is asked to help decide Irashi and Ippty's fate. When Atmos leaves despite promising to carry the group across the Deadly Desert, she worries for Oz and the journey, but returns safely to the Emerald City on a Rash hurry-cane.

Narrative, section by section

6 sections; each heading opens the text

CHAPTER 2

Ozma hosts Betsy's birthday party at the royal residence and gently tries to stop Scraps from teasing the Hungry Tiger. She later hides with Dorothy, sees the flash caused by the hurry-cane, and believes a thunderstorm is coming.

CHAPTER 14

Princess Ozma searches for Betsy Bobbin and is unexpectedly carried into the sky by Atmos Fere. She tries to persuade him to return her to Oz, then punctures him with an emerald pin to escape. After falling back to earth with Atmos, she ties a ribbon around his neck to keep the air in his head and is taken by Rusty Ore to his shop.

CHAPTER 15

Ozma explains the Earth and its inhabitants to Atmos, helps Rusty monitor Atmos's inflation, and accepts Rusty's help in returning home. She travels with Atmos toward the Emerald City, survives the storm and dog attack, and is carried through the great rolling country of the east.

CHAPTER 18

Ozma meets Betsy and her companions after becoming separated and lost with Atmos. She hears the stories of their adventures and praises Reddy as wise and brave, saying he deserves to rule a kingdom. She advises returning to the Emerald City of Oz to consult the Wizard of Oz and then joins the group heading east.

CHAPTER 19

Ozma travels with Reddy and the others and witnesses the conquest of Rash. She helps celebrate Reddy's victory and is asked by Carter Green, together with Reddy, to decide what should be done with Irashi and Ippty.

CHAPTER 20

Ozma tries to stop Atmos Fere from leaving because he had promised to carry her and the others across the desert and back to Oz. After he departs with Ippty and Irashi, she worries about crossing the Deadly Desert, but she returns safely to the Emerald City on a Rash hurry-cane.

Ozma in The Gnome King of Oz

Ruth Plumly Thompson · 1927

Ozma is a little girl fairy, Queen and princess who rules Oz and the Emerald City from her palace in the Emerald City. Called Oz’s wisest and gentlest sovereign, she lives among the city’s residents and court celebrities, inviting nearly a hundred celebrities from her four fairy kingdoms. Her authority and magical resources matter most: she owns the Magic Picture, the magic belt given by Dorothy and rightfully hers, and other magic appliances. She is also responsible for her subjects, including Scraps, and promises to revise Patch’s laws. While discussing curtains with Dorothy, Ozma hears Scraps scream but does not witness the kidnapping; Scraps hopes Ozma can locate her through the Magic Picture. Ruggedo’s central threat is to recover the belt, transform Ozma into a canary, and cage her, prompting Peter’s effort to warn and defend her. Ozma visits Glinda overnight, taking the belt and her magic with her, then returns for a palace party unaware that Ruggedo awaits. Alerted by the oztrich’s roars, she protects the belt, magic box, wishing pills, and egg together, but Ruggedo obtains them when the egg hatches. The Wizard later recovers the box and pills for Ozma. She gives Ozwold an emerald necklace for the baby oztrich’s hatchday. With the belt restored, she offers Peter a princedom, transports him and pirate gold to Philadelphia, and frees Ruggedo after judging seven speechless years sufficient punishment and having him dipped in the fountain of Oblivion.

Narrative, section by section

9 sections; each heading opens the text

CHAPTER 2 The New Queen Of the Quilties

Ozma is the little girl fairy who rules Oz and is described as its wisest and gentlest sovereign. She is in the palace with Dorothy discussing curtains when Scraps's scream is heard, but she does not see the kidnapping.

CHAPTER 3 Duties Of the Quilty Queen

Ozma of Oz is Scraps’s ruler, and Scraps identifies herself as one of Ozma’s free subjects. Scraps believes Ozma may locate her by looking in the Magic Picture.

CHAPTER 6 Ruggedo Discovers Pirate’s Treasure

Ozma is in the Emerald City and possesses or has custody of Ruggedo's magic belt. Ruggedo threatens to steal the belt, turn Ozma into a canary, and imprison her in a gold cage.

CHAPTER 9 Queen Scraps Meets Peter

Ozma is identified as being in danger from Ruggedo’s plans. Peter wants the repaired magic cloak so he can reach the Emerald City and warn her, and he later dreams of defending her.

CHAPTER 16 Kuma’s Hand Is At Work Again

Ozma is to receive a warning about Ruggedo in the Emerald City. Wumbo expects her to use her magic to overpower Ruggedo after Kuma Party's arm finds and holds him.

CHAPTER 17 Mystery In The Emerald City

Ozma spends the night with Glinda and takes her magic belt and other magic appliances with her. She returns to the palace at three o’clock for the party, unaware that Ruggedo is waiting to seize her belt. She stands in the doorway as the Scarecrow begins the welcoming speech.

CHAPTER 18 The Theft Of The Magic Belt

Ozma is startled by the oztrich’s roars and realizes that someone is trying to steal her magic belt. She places the belt, magic box, and wishing pills together with the oztrich egg for protection, but Ruggedo later seizes them when the egg hatches.

CHAPTER 19 The Wizard Makes The Gnome King Visible

Ozma receives the box of mixed magic and the wishing pills after the Wizard removes them from Ruggedo. She gives Ozwold an emerald necklace as a hatchday present for his baby oztrich.

CHAPTER 20 Peter Is Made A Prince Of Oz

Ozma offers Peter a princedom in Oz, then uses her magic belt to transport him and the pirate’s gold to Philadelphia. She explains that the gold is real and later frees Ruggedo after having him dipped in the fountain of Oblivion.

Ozma in The Giant Horse of Oz

Ruth Plumly Thompson · 1928

Ozma is a little fairy and the Queen and ruler of Oz, the Emerald City, and the royal court. She is presented as a beneficent authority whose subjects expect her help: Philador is directed to seek her, and the Scarecrow warns that she may come with her army for important subjects in danger. Before she appears, Akbad predicts that she will travel to the Ozure Isles, destroy Quiberon, and restore Cheeriobed's Queen, while others await her arrival. In the Emerald City, she welcomes Philador and his companions, hears their troubles, uses the magic picture to see Quiberon, and orders the Wizard to take the group to the Ozure Isles. There she listens even to Akbad, favors pardoning him because he helped save Queen Orin and suffered, and permits removal of his golden wings. She secretly works with the Wizard to restore the sea horses and persuades the King, Queen, and little Prince to return to the Emerald City. At her castle, she hosts a banquet, establishes Cheeriobed and Orin as King and Queen of the East, appoints Joe and Hyacinth as rulers of the North, and adds Benny and Herby to her court.

Narrative, section by section

9 sections; each heading opens the text

CHAPTER 3

The Queen of Oz is the ruler of the magic monarchy in the fairy Kingdom of Oz. The Scarecrow plans to present Benny to her in the Emerald City.

CHAPTER 6

Akbad claims that Ozma will travel to the Ozure Isles after the Wizard returns from the blue forest, destroy Quiberon, and restore Cheeriobed's Queen. Cheeriobed prepares to welcome her, although Akbad expects that she will not appear.

CHAPTER 8

The magic slate directs Philador to go to the Emerald City because Ozma of Oz will help him.

CHAPTER 11

The Scarecrow identifies Trot, Benny, and himself as important subjects of Ozma of Oz and warns that she will come with her army if they fail to return. King Silly the Second replies that she cannot fight shadows.

CHAPTER 16

Ozma is expected to come to the Ozure Isles after the Wizard of Oz returns from the blue forest. Trot and her friends are expected to persuade her to come, but she has not arrived by the time Queen Orin returns.

CHAPTER 17

Ozma welcomes Philador and his companions, listens to the account of his troubles, and orders the group to use the magic picture. After seeing Quiberon, she quickly directs the Wizard to transport everyone to the Ozure Isles.

CHAPTER 18

Ozma expresses surprise because she has never heard of Akbad. She urges Cheeriobed to let Akbad speak, supports pardoning him because he helped save Queen Orin and suffered for it, and agrees to let the Wizard remove his golden wings.

CHAPTER 19

Ozma secretly confers with the Wizard and helps arrange the restoration of the sea horses. She persuades the King, Queen, and little Prince to return with the party to the Emerald City.

CHAPTER 20

Ozma welcomes the returning company to her castle and hosts the banquet. She declares Cheeriobed and Orin King and Queen of the East, appoints Joe and Hyacinth rulers of the North, and makes Benny and Herby members of her court.

Ozma in Jack Pumpkinhead of Oz

Ruth Plumly Thompson · 1929

Ozma is the little girl fairy ruler, Queen, Princess, and sovereign of Oz, ruling from the Emerald City. She is described as gracious and is closely supported by Dorothy, Betsy Bobbin, and Trot; she also practices powerful magic, wears a magic belt and emerald crown, and forbids magic in Oz. Jack Pumpkinhead mistakenly considers her his parent. Ozma was once a boy named Tip until Glinda transformed Tip back into Ozma after Mombi's enchantment. When Mogodore attacks the Emerald City, he captures and binds her while she is playing with friends, threatens to force her into marriage, and seats her beside him as his intended bride. Though frightened and distressed, Ozma remains composed, urges her people to be patient and brave, trusts that aid may come from Glinda, and warns Mogodore that her Wizard and Army will defeat him. After Mogodore and his followers are shrunk by the forbidden flagon, Ozma is restored to her rightful place, with her belt and crown returned. She commands the Scares back to Scare City, recognizes Jack Pumpkinhead, Peter, Snif, and Belfaygor as saviors of Oz, sends Mogodore's tiny followers back to Baffleburg, and bids Peter farewell while inviting him to return.

Narrative, section by section

8 sections; each heading opens the text

CHAPTER 1

Ozma is the little girl ruler of Oz and Peter hopes to see her again. Jack explains that Ozma was once a boy named Tip, who was transformed back into Ozma by Glinda after Mombi had enchanted him.

CHAPTER 11

Ozma is in the Emerald City and may not know that Mogodore is coming. Peter fears that without warning, Ozma and the Wizard will not have time to begin their magic before Mogodore captures them.

CHAPTER 12

Ozma is in danger because Mogodore is going to the Emerald City to capture it. Peter and his companions intend to warn or save her.

CHAPTER 17

Ozma plays blind-man's buff with her friends and courtiers in the garden of Ozma's palace. Mogodore's soldiers capture and bind her to a tree, but she remains composed, tells her people to be patient and brave, and warns Mogodore that her Wizard and Army will destroy him. Mogodore declares that he intends to marry her.

CHAPTER 18

Ozma is released from her chains and escorted into the banquet hall, where Mogodore forces her to sit beside him and declares that she will be his bride. She is frightened and distressed by the soldiers' behavior, hopes Scraps and Glinda will help, and rushes forward when Jack Pumpkinhead appears.

CHAPTER 19

Ozma is rescued after the forbidden flagon shrinks Mogodore and his followers. Betsy fastens the magic belt around her waist, Scraps places the emerald crown on her head, and the Scarecrow and Tin Woodman conduct her back to her rightful place at the head of the table.

CHAPTER 20

Ozma commands the Scares to return to Scare City, and they obey at once. After hearing the rescuers' story, she declares that Jack Pumpkinhead, Peter, Snif, and Belfaygor have saved the Kingdom of Oz.

CHAPTER 21

Ozma explains that Mogodore and his followers are now too small to harm anyone and sends them back to their tiny red city in Baffleburg. She says goodbye to Peter and invites him to return to Oz soon and often.

Ozma in The Yellow Knight of Oz

Ruth Plumly Thompson · 1930

Ozma is a fairy girl, Queen and supreme sovereign of Oz, ruling its four countries wisely and keeping its people contented and happy. She lives in her palace among celebrated subjects and gay courtiers, with Princess Dorothy as her best friend and advisor, and owns a magic belt. When the Comfortable Camel, Sir Hokus, and even her magic picture disappear, she receives Tuzzle courteously and permits the camel to return to the Sultan if it wishes. Later, Ozma and Dorothy reach Corabia after a searchlight finds the missing travelers. Recognized there as supreme sovereign, Ozma summons the Sultan of Samandra, removes his magic powers, sends him home, and returns Speedy to Uncle Billy's workshop after Marygolden's marriage.

Narrative, section by section

5 sections; each heading opens the text

CHAPTER ONE

Ozma is described as the powerful and important fairy ruler of Oz, governing its four countries wisely and keeping its people contented and happy. Her palace houses many celebrated subjects, including Dorothy, Bettsy, and Trot, and Ozma's people become involved in the preparations for Sir Hokus's quest.

CHAPTER FIVE

Queen Ozma of Oz is identified as the ruler whose Royal Stable in the Emerald City contains the lost camel. Tuzzle also carries embroidered shawls intended as gifts for her.

CHAPTER SIX

Ozma receives Tuzzle courteously and agrees that the Comfortable Camel may return to the Sultan if it belongs to him and wishes to go. After discovering that the camel and Sir Hokus are missing, she uses the magic picture, only to find that it too has disappeared.

CHAPTER NINETEEN

Ozma arrives suddenly in a blinding flash of lightning with Princess Dorothy. The Corabians and their rulers greet her as the Supreme Sovereign of their magic and mysterious country.

CHAPTER TWENTY

Ozma travels with Dorothy to Corabia after the searchlight locates the Comfortable Camel and Sir Hokus. She summons the Sultan of Samandra, removes his magic powers, and sends him back to Samandra. She returns Speedy to Uncle Billy's workshop after Marygolden's marriage.
